open
AssetServer.java
PLAIN
package com.addonstation.app;

import android.content.res.AssetManager;

import java.io.BufferedReader;
import java.io.ByteArrayOutputStream;
import java.io.IOException;
import java.io.InputStream;
import java.io.InputStreamReader;
import java.io.OutputStream;
import java.net.InetAddress;
import java.net.ServerSocket;
import java.net.Socket;
import java.net.URLDecoder;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;

/**
 * خادم HTTP محلي بسيط (127.0.0.1) يشغّل موقع AddonStation من مجلد الأصول
 * ويحقن إعدادات Supabase في كل صفحة HTML قبل تشغيلها.
 */
public class AssetServer {
    private final String supabaseUrl;
    private final String supabaseKey;
    private final AssetManager assets;
    private ServerSocket socket;
    private volatile boolean running = true;

    public AssetServer(String supabaseUrl, String supabaseKey, AssetManager assets) {
        this.supabaseUrl = supabaseUrl;
        this.supabaseKey = supabaseKey;
        this.assets = assets;
    }

    public void start(int port) throws IOException {
        socket = new ServerSocket(port, 64, InetAddress.getByName("127.0.0.1"));
        Thread t = new Thread(() -> {
            while (running) {
                try (Socket c = socket.accept()) {
                    handle(c);
                } catch (Exception ignored) {
                }
            }
        }, "addonstation-asset-server");
        t.setDaemon(true);
        t.start();
    }

    public void stop() {
        running = false;
        try { if (socket != null) socket.close(); } catch (Exception ignored) {}
    }

    private void handle(Socket c) throws IOException {
        c.setSoTimeout(15000);
        BufferedReader in = new BufferedReader(new InputStreamReader(c.getInputStream(), StandardCharsets.ISO_8859_1));
        String line = in.readLine();
        if (line == null || line.isEmpty()) return;
        String[] parts = line.split(" ");
        boolean headOnly = parts.length > 0 && "HEAD".equalsIgnoreCase(parts[0]);
        String path = parts.length > 1 ? parts[1] : "/";

        String h;
        while ((h = in.readLine()) != null && !h.isEmpty()) {}

        String p = path;
        int q = p.indexOf('?');
        if (q >= 0) p = p.substring(0, q);
        int f = p.indexOf('#');
        if (f >= 0) p = p.substring(0, f);
        try { p = URLDecoder.decode(p, "UTF-8"); } catch (Exception ignored) {}

        String file = resolve(p);
        OutputStream out = c.getOutputStream();
        if (file == null) {
            byte[] body = notFoundPage();
            send(out, "text/html; charset=utf-8", body, 404, headOnly);
            return;
        }
        byte[] body;
        try { body = readAsset(file); } catch (IOException e) {
            send(out, "text/html; charset=utf-8", notFoundPage(), 404, headOnly);
            return;
        }
        String mime = mimeFor(file);
        if (mime.contains("html")) {
            body = injectConfig(body);
        }
        send(out, mime, body, 200, headOnly);
    }

    private void send(OutputStream out, String mime, byte[] body, int code, boolean headOnly) throws IOException {
        String status = code == 200 ? "200 OK" : "404 Not Found";
        StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
        sb.append("HTTP/1.1 ").append(status).append("\r\n");
        sb.append("Content-Type: ").append(mime).append("\r\n");
        sb.append("Content-Length: ").append(headOnly ? 0 : body.length).append("\r\n");
        sb.append("Connection: close\r\n");
        sb.append("Cache-Control: no-store\r\n");
        sb.append("\r\n");
        out.write(sb.toString().getBytes(StandardCharsets.ISO_8859_1));
        if (!headOnly) out.write(body);
        out.flush();
    }

    private byte[] injectConfig(byte[] html) {
        String cfg = "<script>window.__ADDONSTATION_CONFIG__=" + jsonConfig() + ";</script>";
        String doc = new String(html, StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
        int idx = doc.indexOf("<head");
        if (idx >= 0) {
            int gt = doc.indexOf('>', idx);
            if (gt >= 0 && gt < idx + 200) {
                doc = doc.substring(0, gt + 1) + cfg + doc.substring(gt + 1);
                return doc.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
            }
        }
        return (cfg + doc).getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
    }

    private String jsonConfig() {
        return "{\"supabaseUrl\":\"" + jsonEscape(supabaseUrl) + "\",\"supabaseKey\":\"" + jsonEscape(supabaseKey) + "\"}";
    }

    private static String jsonEscape(String s) {
        if (s == null) return "";
        StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
        for (char ch : s.toCharArray()) {
            switch (ch) {
                case '\\': sb.append("\\\\"); break;
                case '"': sb.append("\\\""); break;
                case '\n': sb.append("\\n"); break;
                case '\r': sb.append("\\r"); break;
                case '\t': sb.append("\\t"); break;
                default: sb.append(ch);
            }
        }
        return sb.toString();
    }

    private String resolve(String p) {
        if (p == null || p.contains("..")) return null;
        String rel = p.startsWith("/") ? p.substring(1) : p;
        if (rel.isEmpty()) rel = "index.html";
        String base = "www";
        String candidate = base + "/" + rel;
        if (assetExists(candidate)) return candidate;
        if (!rel.contains(".")) {
            if (assetExists(base + "/" + rel + "/index.html")) return base + "/" + rel + "/index.html";
            if (assetExists(base + "/" + rel + ".html")) return base + "/" + rel + ".html";
        }
        if (rel.endsWith("/") && assetExists(base + "/" + rel + "index.html")) return base + "/" + rel + "index.html";
        return null;
    }

    private boolean assetExists(String name) {
        try {
            InputStream in = assets.open(name);
            in.close();
            return true;
        } catch (IOException e) {
            return false;
        }
    }

    private byte[] readAsset(String name) throws IOException {
        InputStream in = assets.open(name);
        ByteArrayOutputStream bos = new ByteArrayOutputStream();
        byte[] buf = new byte[8192];
        int n;
        while ((n = in.read(buf)) > 0) bos.write(buf, 0, n);
        in.close();
        return bos.toByteArray();
    }

    private byte[] notFoundPage() {
        String html = "<html dir='rtl'><body style='background:#111720;color:#fff;font-family:sans-serif;text-align:center;padding:40px'>"
                + "<h2>404 — الصفحة غير موجودة</h2><p>أعد المحاولة من القائمة الرئيسية</p></body></html>";
        return html.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
    }

    private static String mimeFor(String file) {
        String lower = file.toLowerCase();
        if (lower.endsWith(".html")) return "text/html; charset=utf-8";
        if (lower.endsWith(".js")) return "application/javascript; charset=utf-8";
        if (lower.endsWith(".mjs")) return "application/javascript; charset=utf-8";
        if (lower.endsWith(".css")) return "text/css; charset=utf-8";
        if (lower.endsWith(".json")) return "application/json; charset=utf-8";
        if (lower.endsWith(".svg")) return "image/svg+xml";
        if (lower.endsWith(".png")) return "image/png";
        if (lower.endsWith(".jpg") || lower.endsWith(".jpeg")) return "image/jpeg";
        if (lower.endsWith(".webp")) return "image/webp";
        if (lower.endsWith(".gif")) return "image/gif";
        if (lower.endsWith(".ico")) return "image/x-icon";
        if (lower.endsWith(".woff2")) return "font/woff2";
        if (lower.endsWith(".woff")) return "font/woff";
        if (lower.endsWith(".ttf")) return "font/ttf";
        if (lower.endsWith(".mp4")) return "video/mp4";
        if (lower.endsWith(".webm")) return "video/webm";
        if (lower.endsWith(".mp3")) return "audio/mpeg";
        if (lower.endsWith(".ogg")) return "audio/ogg";
        if (lower.endsWith(".wav")) return "audio/wav";
        if (lower.endsWith(".xml")) return "text/xml; charset=utf-8";
        if (lower.endsWith(".txt")) return "text/plain; charset=utf-8";
        if (lower.endsWith(".pdf")) return "application/pdf";
        if (lower.endsWith(".zip")) return "application/zip";
        if (lower.endsWith(".mcpack")) return "application/octet-stream";
        if (lower.endsWith(".mcworld")) return "application/octet-stream";
        return "application/octet-stream";
    }
}
Open
الآن شاشة الإعداد الأولى (حيث يدخل المستخدم بيانات Supabase العامة) والواجهة الرئيسية:

MainActivity.java
PLAIN
package com.addonstation.app;

import android.app.Activity;
import android.content.Intent;
import android.content.SharedPreferences;
import android.graphics.Color;
import android.net.Uri;
import android.os.Bundle;
import android.view.Gravity;
import android.view.View;
import android.view.ViewGroup;
import android.webkit.ValueCallback;
import android.webkit.WebChromeClient;
import android.webkit.WebResourceError;
import android.webkit.WebResourceRequest;
import android.webkit.WebSettings;
import android.webkit.WebView;
import android.webkit.WebViewClient;
import android.widget.Button;
import android.widget.FrameLayout;
import android.widget.Toast;

public class MainActivity extends Activity {

    static final String PREFS = "addonstation_cfg";
    static final String KEY_URL = "supabaseUrl";
    static final String KEY_API = "supabaseKey";

    private static final int FILE_CHOOSER_REQUEST = 1001;
    private static final int PORT = 8123;

    private WebView web;
    private AssetServer server;
    private String currentUrl = "";
    private String currentKey = "";
    private ValueCallback<Uri[]> pendingUpload;

    @Override
    protected void onCreate(Bundle savedInstanceState) {
        super.onCreate(savedInstanceState);
        SharedPreferences prefs = getSharedPreferences(PREFS, MODE_PRIVATE);
        currentUrl = prefs.getString(KEY_URL, "");
        currentKey = prefs.getString(KEY_API, "");

        if (currentUrl.isEmpty() || currentKey.isEmpty()) {
            Intent i = new Intent(this, ConfigActivity.class);
            i.putExtra("reason", "setup");
            startActivity(i);
            finish();
            return;
        }
        buildUi();
    }

    @Override
    protected void onResume() {
        super.onResume();
        if (web == null) return;
        // قد يكون المستخدم عدّل الإعدادات من شاشة ⚙
        SharedPreferences prefs = getSharedPreferences(PREFS, MODE_PRIVATE);
        String url = prefs.getString(KEY_URL, "");
        String key = prefs.getString(KEY_API, "");
        if (!url.equals(currentUrl) || !key.equals(currentKey)) {
            currentUrl = url;
            currentKey = key;
            if (server != null) server.stop();
            startServer();
            web.reload();
        }
    }

    private void startServer() {
        server = new AssetServer(currentUrl, currentKey, getAssets());
        try {
            server.start(PORT);
        } catch (Exception e) {
            Toast.makeText(this, "تعذر تشغيل الخادم: " + e.getMessage(), Toast.LENGTH_LONG).show();
        }
    }

    private void buildUi() {
        startServer();

        FrameLayout root = new FrameLayout(this);
        root.setBackgroundColor(Color.parseColor("#111720"));

        web = new WebView(this);
        web.setBackgroundColor(Color.parseColor("#111720"));
        WebSettings s = web.getSettings();
        s.setJavaScriptEnabled(true);
        s.setDomStorageEnabled(true);
        s.setDatabaseEnabled(true);
        s.setAllowFileAccess(true);
        s.setMediaPlaybackRequiresUserGesture(false);
        s.setLoadWithOverviewMode(true);
        s.setUseWideViewPort(true);
        s.setSupportZoom(false);
        s.setTextZoom(100);

        web.setWebViewClient(new WebViewClient() {
            @Override
            public void onReceivedError(WebView v, WebResourceRequest req, WebResourceError err) {
                if (req.isForMainFrame()) {
                    String msg = "تعذر الاتصال بالخادم المحلي";
                    String html = "<html dir='rtl'><body style='background:#111720;color:#fff;font-family:sans-serif;text-align:center;padding:50px 20px'>"
                            + "<h2 style='color:#4da53c'>AddonStation</h2>"
                            + "<p>" + msg + "</p>"
                            + "<button onclick='location.reload()' style='padding:14px 30px;background:#4da53c;color:#fff;border:0;border-radius:10px;font-size:16px'>إعادة المحاولة</button>"
                            + "</body></html>";
                    v.loadDataWithBaseURL(null, html, "text/html", "utf-8", null);
                }
            }
        });

        web.setWebChromeClient(new WebChromeClient() {
            @Override
            public boolean onShowFileChooser(WebView wv, ValueCallback<Uri[]> callback, FileChooserParams params) {
                if (pendingUpload != null) pendingUpload.onReceiveValue(null);
                pendingUpload = callback;
                Intent i = new Intent(Intent.ACTION_GET_CONTENT);
                i.addCategory(Intent.CATEGORY_OPENABLE);
                i.setType("*/*");
                try {
                    startActivityForResult(Intent.createChooser(i, "اختر صورة أو ملفًا"), FILE_CHOOSER_REQUEST);
                    return true;
                } catch (Exception e) {
                    pendingUpload = null;
                    return false;
                }
            }
        });

        // تحميل الملفات (إضافات .mcpack وغيرها) تفتح في متصفح خارجي
        web.setDownloadListener((url, userAgent, contentDisposition, mimetype, contentLength) -> {
            try {
                startActivity(new Intent(Intent.ACTION_VIEW, Uri.parse(url)));
            } catch (Exception ignored) {
            }
        });

        web.loadUrl("http://127.0.0.1:" + PORT + "/");
        root.addView(web, new FrameLayout.LayoutParams(
                ViewGroup.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT, ViewGroup.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT));

        // زر إعدادات صغير شفاف (لإعادة ضبط بيانات الاتصال عند الحاجة)
        Button gear = new Button(this);
        gear.setText("⚙");
        gear.setTextSize(18);
        gear.setTextColor(Color.WHITE);
        gear.setAlpha(0.45f);
        gear.setBackgroundColor(Color.parseColor("#00000000"));
        FrameLayout.LayoutParams gp = new FrameLayout.LayoutParams(dp(44), dp(44));
        gp.gravity = Gravity.TOP | Gravity.START;
        gp.topMargin = dp(10);
        gp.leftMargin = dp(10);
        gear.setLayoutParams(gp);
        gear.setOnClickListener(v -> startActivity(new Intent(this, ConfigActivity.class)));
        root.addView(gear);

        setContentView(root);
    }

    @Override
    protected void onActivityResult(int requestCode, int resultCode, Intent data) {
        if (requestCode == FILE_CHOOSER_REQUEST) {
            if (pendingUpload == null) return;
            Uri[] result = (resultCode == RESULT_OK && data != null && data.getData() != null)
                    ? new Uri[]{data.getData()}
                    : null;
            pendingUpload.onReceiveValue(result);
            pendingUpload = null;
        } else {
            super.onActivityResult(requestCode, resultCode, data);
        }
    }

    @Override
    public void onBackPressed() {
        if (web != null && web.canGoBack()) {
            web.goBack();
        } else {
            super.onBackPressed();
        }
    }

    @Override
    protected void onDestroy() {
        if (server != null) server.stop();
        if (web != null) web.destroy();
        super.onDestroy();
    }

    private int dp(int v) {
        return Math.round(v * getResources().getDisplayMetrics().density);
    }
}

لما سجلت الدخول قالي invalid login credentials

The user is getting "invalid login credentials" when trying to log in. Let me think about what could cause this.

The user is using the Android APK app we just built. They logged in and got "invalid login credentials". This is a Supabase auth error - it means the login request reached Supabase but the email/password combination was rejected.

Possible causes:

They typed the wrong email or password (most common)
The password they use locally (on web) works because... hmm, actually the credentials should be the same since it's the same Supabase project.
Maybe in the app they entered the URL or key incorrectly — but if the URL/key were wrong, they'd get a different error like "Failed to fetch" or "Invalid API key", not "invalid login credentials".
Actually "Invalid login credentials" is the exact Supabase error message when email+password don't match. So the connection to Supabase works, but the credentials are wrong.

Possible scenarios:

The user's account was created on the web version with a password, and now they typed it wrong (typo, different keyboard layout — Arabic keyboard typing password, auto-correct, uppercase/lowercase).
They never created an account with that email; maybe they registered but the account was created differently.
Maybe they created a new account inside the app and then tried to log in with wrong password.
Important context: The user is Arabic, non-programmer, on mobile. Password fields with Arabic keyboards often cause issues — e.g., the password contains Latin characters but they type on an Arabic keyboard layout. Or maybe auto-capitalization.

Also — another possibility: in the app, when they first opened it, they might have registered (sign up) a new account. But wait — did they log in to the web version before? They have a local dev site. They must have accounts. The admin email is muhammadalfarjani5@gmail.com.

Hmm, but there's another subtle issue: On the login page of the site, maybe the login form sends to Supabase fine. "Invalid login credentials" is the standard message.

Also possible: The user may be entering their password with extra spaces, or the email with spaces.

Another important thing: Maybe they registered inside the app but email confirmation is on? If email confirmation is required, the user can't log in until they confirm email — the error would be "Email not confirmed" though, not "invalid login credentials". So that's not it.

Wait — actually there's another possibility: the password recovery/reset. Or maybe they created the account before with the local dev server which connected to the same Supabase. So password should be the same.

Most likely: typo in password, or Arabic keyboard layout issue on Android.

What can I do to help?

Give simple steps:
Check email spelling (lowercase, no spaces)
Check password: Latin letters only, Caps Lock, numbers; try typing in English keyboard

If they never registered in the app, register a new account.

That requires the Supabase project to have email service enabled (default uses Supabase's email, works without SMTP config? Actually Supabase sends emails via their default service if no custom SMTP — yes, default emails work).

Now I understand the login/register flow. Let me now rewrite login.astro with:

Then update Android app:

Manifest: intent-filter for addonstation:// scheme on MainActivity
MainActivity: handle onNewIntent + onCreate intent → if URI has scheme addonstation, extract # fragment (token/type) and load into WebView: http://127.0.0.1:8123/login#access_token=...&type=recovery...
Wait, how does supabase-js recovery link look? The email link is: https://<ref>.supabase.co/auth/v1/verify?token=<jwt>&type=recovery&redirect_to=<redirectTo>.

Supabase's hosted verify page: when opened, it redirects to redirect_to with the tokens appended in the hash: redirect_to#access_token=...&expires_in=...&token_type=bearer&type=recovery.

So flow: user clicks email link (https://.supabase.co/auth/v1/verify?token=...&type=recovery&redirect_to=addonstation://recovery) → browser opens (their default browser, NOT the app, since it's an https link) → supabase.co verify page processes → redirects to addonstation://recovery#access_token=...&type=recovery → Android system sees scheme addonstation → opens our app (MainActivity with the intent-filter) → we get the URI with fragment → we load http://127.0.0.1:8123/login#access_token=...&type=recovery in the WebView → supabase-js (detectSessionInUrl true) picks up the hash, exchanges, fires PASSWORD_RECOVERY event → login page shows new-password form → user sets new password → updateUser → success → redirect to /profile.

Note: The intent URI fragment — when Android delivers the intent, Uri.getFragment() returns the fragment. We need to pass full "http://127.0.0.1:8123/login#" + fragment.

Also onNewIntent handling: if app is already running, MainActivity is singleTop? It's not declared singleTop. With FLAG_ACTIVITY... default launchMode standard → a new instance would be created. The intent-filter on MainActivity with VIEW action + addonstation scheme + category BROWSABLE + DEFAULT. Also set android:launchMode="singleTask" so if running, onNewIntent is called.

Implementation in MainActivity:

java


@Override
protected void onCreate(Bundle savedInstanceState) {
    ...
    String recoveryHash = extractRecoveryHash(getIntent());
    ...
    web.loadUrl("http://127.0.0.1:" + PORT + "/" + (recoveryHash != null ? "login#" + recoveryHash : ""));
}
@Override
protected void onNewIntent(Intent intent) {
    super.onNewIntent(intent);
    setIntent(intent);
    String h = extractRecoveryHash(intent);
    if (h != null && web != null) {
        web.loadUrl("http://127.0.0.1:" + PORT + "/login#" + h);
    }
}
private String extractRecoveryHash(Intent intent) {
    if (intent == null) return null;
    Uri uri = intent.getData();
    if (uri == null) return null;
    String scheme = uri.getScheme();
    if (scheme == null || !scheme.equalsIgnoreCase("addonstation")) return null;
    String f = uri.getFragment();
    return f != null && !f.isEmpty() ? f : null;
}
But careful: onCreate flow — if no config (first run) we go to ConfigActivity; the recovery intent would be lost after config. Acceptable edge case; after config they can request reset again.

Also there's the timing issue: web loads login page, supabase-js processes hash → fine.

One important detail: supabase-js detectSessionInUrl — default true. Good.

Another detail: the redirectTo we pass must be registered: in Supabase dashboard, "Redirect URLs" — for custom schemes, Supabase requires the redirect URL to be added to the allowlist: Authentication → URL Configuration → Redirect URLs → add addonstation://recovery. Otherwise the verify page may block the redirect. Actually, for custom schemes, Supabase blocks unlisted redirect URLs ("Redirect URL is not allowed"). So I must tell the user to add addonstation://recovery to Redirect URLs. I'll include that instruction with simple steps.

For the web version (PC), redirectTo: we can leave default (Site URL localhost:3001) → works when dev server runs on PC.

import { supabase, supabaseReady } from '~/lib/supabase.js';
const form = document.querySelector('#loginForm');
const message = document.querySelector('#formMessage');
const isApp = typeof window !== 'undefined' && !!window.__ADDONSTATION_CONFIG__;
const arErrors = {
  'Invalid login credentials': 'البريد الإلكتروني أو كلمة المرور غير صحيحة. تأكد من كتابتهما بالشكل الصحيح (بدون فراغات، لوحة مفاتيح إنجليزية لكلمة المرور).',
  'Email not confirmed': 'لم يتم تأكيد بريدك الإلكتروني بعد. افتح رابط التأكيد المرسل إلى بريدك.',
  'User already registered': 'هذا البريد مسجل بالفعل — سجل الدخول مباشرة.',
  'Password should be at least 6 characters': 'كلمة المرور يجب أن تكون 6 أحرف على الأقل.',
  'Unable to validate email address': 'صيغة البريد الإلكتروني غير صحيحة.',
};
const friendly = err => arErrors[err] || (err && err.includes('login') ? 'البريد أو كلمة المرور غير صحيحة.' : err);

Actually the hash-based detection runs on load; the event fires after session set. Both fine — use both: on load check hash; also subscribe.

Careful with RTL direction: page is RTL already (Arabic). Fine.

Also note: supabase.auth.updateUser requires the session from the recovery token — supabase-js processes the URL automatically. There can be a race: page loads, supabase-js hasn't processed hash yet when user clicks submit. Rare; the user types a password first, so enough time.

Also the "form-message" color: red for errors; add success class. I'll add a .form-message.success class with green color, or just set color inline.

Let me also make the forgot link styled nicely.

    <header class="account-topbar"><a href="/" class="account-brand"><span class="account-logo-wrap"><img src="/imgs/minecraft-logo.webp" alt="Minecraft" /></span><span class="account-brand-divider"></span><span class="accountstation-brand"><strong>AddonStation</strong><small>STORE</small></span></a><a class="account-back" href="/addons"><span data-i18n="account.back">العودة إلى المتجر</span></a></header>

<script>
  import { supabase, supabaseReady } from '~/lib/supabase.js';
  const isApp = typeof window !== 'undefined' && !!window.__ADDONSTATION_CONFIG__;

  const form = document.querySelector('#loginForm');
  const message = document.querySelector('#formMessage');

  /* ترجمة أخطاء Supabase الشائعة إلى العربية */
  const arErrors = {
    'Invalid login credentials': 'البريد الإلكتروني أو كلمة المرور غير صحيحة. تأكد من كتابتهما بالشكل الصحيح: البريد بدون فراغات، وكلمة المرور بلوحة مفاتيح إنجليزية (الحرف الكبير A يختلف عن a).',
    'Email not confirmed': 'لم يتم تأكيد بريدك الإلكتروني بعد. افتح رابط التأكيد الذي وصل إلى بريدك، ثم سجّل الدخول.',
    'User already registered': 'هذا البريد مسجل بالفعل — سجل الدخول مباشرة.',
    'Password should be at least 6 characters': 'كلمة المرور يجب أن تكون 6 أحرف على الأقل.',
    'Unable to validate email address': 'صيغة البريد الإلكتروني غير صحيحة.',
    'Signups not allowed for this instance': 'التسجيل مغلق حاليًا في إعدادات Supabase.',
    'Request Failed': 'تعذر الاتصال بالخادم. تأكد من اتصال الإنترنت.',
    'NetworkError when attempting to fetch resource': 'تعذر الاتصال بالإنترنت. تحقق من الاتصال وأعد المحاولة.'
  };
  const friendly = (err) => {
    if (!err) return '';
    if (arErrors[err.message]) return arErrors[err.message];
    if (err.message && /login|credential/i.test(err.message)) return 'البريد الإلكتروني أو كلمة المرور غير صحيحة.';
    return err.message || 'حدث خطأ غير متوقع';
  };

  /* -------- استعادة كلمة المرور ---------- */
  const forgotLink = document.querySelector('#forgotLink');
  const forgotBox = document.querySelector('#forgotBox');
  const forgotEmail = document.querySelector('#forgotEmail');
  const forgotSend = document.querySelector('#forgotSend');
  const forgotMessage = document.querySelector('#forgotMessage');

  forgotLink?.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    forgotBox.hidden = !forgotBox.hidden;
    if (!forgotBox.hidden) forgotEmail.focus();
  });

  forgotSend?.addEventListener('click', async () => {
    if (!supabaseReady) { forgotMessage.textContent = 'لم يتم إعداد Supabase بعد.'; return; }
    const email = forgotEmail.value.trim();
    if (!email) { forgotMessage.textContent = 'أدخل بريدك الإلكتروني أولًا.'; return; }
    forgotMessage.textContent = 'جارٍ إرسال الرابط إلى بريدك...';
    forgotMessage.style.color = '#75d766';
    const opts = isApp ? { redirectTo: 'addonstation://recovery' } : {};
    const { error } = await supabase.auth.resetPasswordForEmail(email, opts);
    if (error) { forgotMessage.style.color = ''; forgotMessage.textContent = friendly(error); return; }
    forgotMessage.textContent = '✅ تم الإرسال! افتح بريدك الآن واضغط على الرابط لإنشاء كلمة مرور جديدة.';
  });

  /* -------- وضع إنشاء كلمة مرور جديدة (رابط الاستعادة) ---------- */
  const recoveryBox = document.querySelector('#recoveryBox');
  const recoveryForm = document.querySelector('#recoveryForm');
  const newPassword = document.querySelector('#newPassword');
  const newPassword2 = document.querySelector('#newPassword2');
  const recoveryMessage = document.querySelector('#recoveryMessage');
  const pageTitle = document.querySelector('#pageTitle');

  const showRecoveryMode = () => {
    document.querySelector('#loginBox').hidden = true;
    document.querySelector('#switchLine').hidden = true;
    recoveryBox.hidden = false;
    pageTitle.textContent = 'إنشاء كلمة مرور جديدة';
    newPassword.focus();
  };

  if (location.hash && location.hash.includes('type=recovery')) showRecoveryMode();
  supabase?.auth.onAuthStateChange((event) => {
    if (event === 'PASSWORD_RECOVERY') showRecoveryMode();
  });

  recoveryForm?.addEventListener('submit', async (event) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    const p1 = newPassword.value;
    const p2 = newPassword2.value;
    recoveryMessage.style.color = '';
    if (p1.length < 6) { recoveryMessage.textContent = 'كلمة المرور يجب أن تكون 6 أحرف على الأقل.'; return; }
    if (p1 !== p2) { recoveryMessage.textContent = 'كلمتا المرور غير متطابقتين — أعد كتابتهما.'; return; }
    recoveryMessage.textContent = 'جارٍ حفظ كلمة المرور...';
    recoveryMessage.style.color = '#75d766';
    const { error } = await supabase.auth.updateUser({ password: p1 });
    if (error) { recoveryMessage.style.color = ''; recoveryMessage.textContent = friendly(error); return; }
    recoveryMessage.textContent = '✅ تم حفظ كلمة المرور الجديدة! جارٍ الدخول إلى حسابك...';
    setTimeout(() => { window.location.href = '/profile'; }, 1500);
  });

  /* -------- تسجيل الدخول العادي ---------- */
  form?.addEventListener('submit', async (event) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    if (!supabaseReady) { message.textContent = 'لم يتم إعداد Supabase بعد.'; return; }
    const { email, password } = Object.fromEntries(new FormData(form));
    message.textContent = window.i18n?.t('auth.login.progress') || 'جارٍ تسجيل الدخول...';
    message.style.color = '#75d766';
    const { error } = await supabase.auth.signInWithPassword({ email, password });
    if (error) { message.style.color = ''; message.textContent = friendly(error); return; }
    window.location.href = '/profile';
  });
</script>

Wait — one consideration about the redirect URL: Supabase requires the redirect URL to be added in the dashboard for the reset email link to redirect to addonstation://recovery. Actually, for non-HTTP redirects, Supabase's allowed redirect list must include it. The default behavior: if redirectTo not allowed, the link may show an error. So the user MUST add addonstation://recovery in Authentication → URL Configuration → Redirect URLs.

Also note: the reset email link flow — supabase.co/auth/v1/verify page redirects to the redirectTo with the tokens in the hash fragment. Then Android opens the app. This works.

Alternatively if the user doesn't add the redirect URL, the email link will still open the supabase verify page and might display an error about redirect URL. I'll include this step clearly.

Redirect rule in Base.astro inline script:

js


(function(){
  try{
    var w=window.innerWidth;
    if(w>=900) return; // desktop
    var p=location.pathname;
    if(p.indexOf('/m/')===0) return;
    if(/^\/(admin|docs|api|_astro|assets|imgs|items|stickers|fonts|css|favicon)/.test(p)) return;
    if(location.pathname==='/' ) location.replace('/m/');
    else location.replace('/m'+p);
  }catch(e){}
})();

I'll do runtime caching: cache successful GET responses (limit 60 entries), network-first for navigations with offline fallback to cached '/' . Keep SW simple and safe:

js


const CACHE='addonstation-v1';
self.addEventListener('install',e=>{e.waitUntil(caches.open(CACHE).then(c=>c.addAll(['/m/','/manifest.webmanifest'])).catch(()=>{}));self.skipWaiting();});
self.addEventListener('activate',e=>{e.waitUntil(caches.keys().then(ks=>Promise.all(ks.filter(k=>k!==CACHE).map(k=>caches.delete(k)))).then(()=>self.clients.claim()));});
self.addEventListener('fetch',e=>{
 const req=e.request; if(req.method!=='GET')return;
 const url=new URL(req.url); if(url.origin!==location.origin)return;
 e.respondWith(
   fetch(req).then(res=>{ const copy=res.clone(); if(res.ok && (url.pathname.endsWith('/') || /\.(js|css|png|jpg|webp|woff2|svg|json)$/.test(url.pathname))) caches.open(CACHE).then(c=>c.put(req,copy)); return res; })
   .catch(()=>caches.match(req).then(m=>m||caches.match('/m/')))
 );
});
Cache 60 limit: use caches with max entries? Skip complexity; runtime cache grows but Chrome quota handles it; add simple size guard via navigator.storage? Keep simple.

كل شيء واضح. الآن أبني التطبيق — أبدأ بأساسات PWA (أيقونات، manifest، Service Worker):


Edited files
2
sw.js
JS
/* AddonStation PWA Service Worker */
const CACHE = 'addonstation-v1';

self.addEventListener('install', (e) => {
  e.waitUntil(
    caches.open(CACHE)
      .then((c) => c.addAll(['/m/', '/manifest.webmanifest']))
      .catch(() => {})
  );
  self.skipWaiting();
});

self.addEventListener('activate', (e) => {
  e.waitUntil(
    caches.keys()
      .then((keys) => Promise.all(keys.filter((k) => k !== CACHE).map((k) => caches.delete(k))))
      .then(() => self.clients.claim())
  );
});

self.addEventListener('fetch', (e) => {
  const req = e.request;
  if (req.method !== 'GET') return;
  const url = new URL(req.url);
  if (url.origin !== location.origin) return;

  // الصفحات (التنقل) — شبكة أولًا ثم الكاش
  if (req.mode === 'navigate') {
    e.respondWith(
      fetch(req)
        .then((res) => {
          const copy = res.clone();
          caches.open(CACHE).then((c) => c.put('/m/', copy));
          return res;
        })
        .catch(() => caches.match('/m/'))
    );
    return;
  }

  // الأصول — كاش أولًا ثم شبكة
  e.respondWith(
    caches.match(req).then((cached) => {
      if (cached) return cached;
      return fetch(req).then((res) => {
        if (res.ok && /\.(js|css|png|jpg|jpeg|webp|gif|svg|woff2?|ttf|json)$/.test(url.pathname)) {
          const copy = res.clone();
          caches.open(CACHE).then((c) => c.put(req, copy));
        }
        return res;
      });
    })
  );
});

mobile.js
JS
// AddonStation Mobile — أدوات مساعدة مشتركة
import { supabase, supabaseReady } from './supabase.js';

export const esc = (v) => String(v || '').replace(/[&<>"']/g, (c) => ({ '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;', "'": '&#39;' }[c]));

export const MENTION_RE = /@([\p{L}\p{N}_.-]+)/gu;

export function renderMentions(text) {
  if (!text) return '';
  return esc(text).replace(MENTION_RE, (m, n) => `<span class="m-mention" data-mention="${esc(n)}">@${n}</span>`);
}

export function fmtTime(ts) {
  if (!ts) return '';
  const d = new Date(ts);
  const now = Date.now();
  const diff = now - d.getTime();
  if (diff < 60000) return 'الآن';
  if (diff < 3600000) return Math.floor(diff / 60000) + ' د';
  const sameDay = new Date(now).toDateString() === d.toDateString();
  if (sameDay) return d.toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' });
  const yesterday = new Date(now - 86400000).toDateString() === d.toDateString();
  if (yesterday) return 'أمس';
  return d.toLocaleDateString([], { day: 'numeric', month: 'numeric' });
}

export function rankLabel(p) {
  if (!p) return '';
  if (p.role === 'admin') return 'TheKing ✦';
  if (p.role === 'moderator') return 'Manager ✦';
  if (p.role === 'creator') return 'Creator';
  if (p.plan === 'epic') return 'Epic 💎';
  if (p.plan === 'legendary') return 'Legendary 👑';
  return 'Free 🍃';
}

export function rankClass(p) {
  if (!p) return 'free';
  if (p.role === 'admin') return 'admin';
  if (p.role === 'moderator') return 'moderator';
  if (p.role === 'creator') return 'creator';
  return p.plan === 'epic' || p.plan === 'legendary' ? p.plan : 'free';
}

export function planName(plan) {
  if (plan === 'epic') return 'Epic 💎';
  if (plan === 'legendary') return 'Legendary 👑';
  return 'Free 🍃';
}

export async function getSession() {
  const { data } = await supabase.auth.getSession();
  return data?.session || null;
}

export function toast(msg, ms = 2400) {
  let t = document.querySelector('.m-toast');
  if (!t) {
    t = document.createElement('div');
    t.className = 'm-toast';
    document.body.appendChild(t);
  }
  t.textContent = msg;
  requestAnimationFrame(() => t.classList.add('show'));
  clearTimeout(t._timer);
  t._timer = setTimeout(() => t.classList.remove('show'), ms);
}

export function openSheet(el) {
  const backdrop = document.querySelector('.m-sheet-backdrop');
  if (backdrop) backdrop.classList.add('open');
  el.classList.add('open');
  document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
}

export function closeSheet(el) {
  const backdrop = document.querySelector('.m-sheet-backdrop');
  if (backdrop) backdrop.classList.remove('open');
  el.classList.remove('open');
  document.body.style.overflow = '';
}

export function initSheet(sheetEl, closeBtn) {
  const backdrop = document.querySelector('.m-sheet-backdrop');
  if (backdrop) backdrop.onclick = () => closeSheet(sheetEl);
  closeBtn?.addEventListener('click', () => closeSheet(sheetEl));
}

export function contentCardHTML(item, wide = false) {
  const rank = item.plan === 'legendary' ? '👑 Legendary' : item.plan === 'epic' ? '💎 Epic' : '🍃 Free';
  const cls = rank.includes('Legendary') ? 'legendary' : rank.includes('Epic') ? 'epic' : 'free';
  const meta = `⭐ ${Number(item.rating_average || 0).toFixed(1)} · ↓ ${item.download_count || 0}`;
  if (wide) {
    return `<a class="m-ccard-h" href="/m/item?slug=${esc(item.slug)}">
      <span class="cc-cover"><img loading="lazy" src="${esc(item.cover_url || '/imgs/project-logo.png')}" alt=""/></span>
      <span style="flex:1;min-width:0"><span class="cc-title">${esc(item.title)}</span>
      <span class="cc-meta">${meta}</span></span></a>`;
  }
  return `<a class="m-ccard m-anim" href="/m/item?slug=${esc(item.slug)}">
    <span class="cc-cover"><img loading="lazy" src="${esc(item.cover_url || '/imgs/project-logo.png')}" alt=""/>
      <span class="cc-plan m-rank ${cls}">${rank}</span></span>
    <span class="cc-body"><span class="cc-title">${esc(item.title)}</span>
      <span class="cc-meta">${meta}</span></span></a>`;
}

export function unreadCount() {
  return supabase.from('chat_notifications').select('id', { count: 'exact', head: true }).eq('is_read', false);
}

<script>
  import { supabase, supabaseReady } from '~/lib/supabase.js';
  import { esc, getSession, contentCardHTML, rankClass, rankLabel, planName } from '~/lib/mobile.js';

  const app = document.querySelector('#homeApp');
  const IMG = (u) => u || '/imgs/project-logo.png';

  (async () => {
    if (!supabaseReady) { app.innerHTML = '<div class="m-empty"><span class="m-empty-ico">⚠️</span><b>لم يتم إعداد Supabase</b></div>'; return; }
    const session = await getSession();
    let profile = null;
    if (session?.user) {
      const { data: p } = await supabase.from('profiles').select('*').eq('id', session.user.id).maybeSingle();
      profile = p;
    }

    const [featured, latest, top] = await Promise.all([
      supabase.from('content_items').select('*').eq('is_published', true).eq('is_featured', true).order('created_at', { ascending: false }).limit(8),
      supabase.from('content_items').select('*').eq('is_published', true).order('created_at', { ascending: false }).limit(6),
      supabase.from('content_items').select('*').eq('is_published', true).order('rating_average', { ascending: false }).limit(6)
    ]);

    const hour = new Date().getHours();
    const greet = hour < 12 ? 'صباح الخير' : hour < 18 ? 'مساء الخير' : 'مساء الخير';
    const feats = featured.data || [];
    const lates = latest.data || [];
    const tops = top.data || [];

    app.innerHTML = `
      <div class="m-anim">
        <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;gap:10px;margin:2px 2px 12px">
          <div>
            <div style="font-size:11px;color:var(--m-muted)">${greet} 👋</div>
            <div style="font-family:var(--m-font);font-size:17px;margin-top:2px">${profile ? esc(profile.username) : 'مرحبًا بك في'}</div>
          </div>
          ${profile ? `<span class="m-rank ${rankClass(profile)}">${rankLabel(profile)}</span>` : `<a class="m-btn m-btn-green" style="min-height:36px;padding:7px 16px;font-size:12px" href="/m/login">دخول</a>`}
        </div>

        ${profile ? planCard(profile) : `<div class="m-card" style="margin-bottom:12px;background:linear-gradient(150deg,rgba(255,212,121,.13),var(--m-surface));border-color:rgba(255,212,121,.35)">
          <b style="font-size:14px">متجر إضافات وعوالم Minecraft 🎮</b>
          <p style="color:var(--m-muted);font-size:12px;margin:6px 0 12px;line-height:1.7">حمّل إضافات وعوالم PS4/PS5، شارك في المجتمع، وانضم إلى العائلة الملكية ✦</p>
          <div style="display:flex;gap:8px">
            <a class="m-btn m-btn-green" style="flex:1;min-height:40px;font-size:12.5px" href="/m/login">تسجيل الدخول</a>
            <a class="m-btn m-btn-ghost" style="flex:1;min-height:40px;font-size:12.5px" href="/m/register">حساب جديد</a>
          </div>
        </div>`}

        <a class="m-search" href="/m/addons" style="display:block;text-decoration:none">
          <span class="m-search-ico">🔍</span>
          <input readonly placeholder="ابحث عن إضافات وعوالم..." style="pointer-events:none"/>
        </a>

        <div class="m-card-h"><h2>⚡ إجراءات سريعة</h2></div>
        <div style="display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(4,1fr);gap:8px">
          ${[['⭐', 'مفضلتي', '/m/profile?tab=favs'], ['📥', 'تحميلاتي', '/m/profile?tab=content'], ['👑', 'الترقية', '/m/profile?tab=plan'], ['🎮', 'الجهاز', '/m/support']].map(([ic, lb, href], i) => `
            <a href="${href}" class="m-anim m-anim-${i + 1}" style="display:flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:center;gap:6px;padding:13px 4px;border-radius:15px;background:var(--m-surface);border:1px solid var(--m-border);text-decoration:none;color:var(--m-text);transition:transform .12s">
              <span style="font-size:21px">${ic}</span>

            </a>`).join('')}
        </div>

        ${feats.length ? `<div class="m-card-h"><h2>⭐ مميز</h2><a href="/m/addons">عرض الكل</a></div>
        <div class="m-hscroll">${feats.map((it, i) => contentCardHTML(it, true)).join('')}</div>` : ''}

        <div class="m-grid">${lates.map((it, i) => contentCardHTML(it)).join('')}</div>` : ''}

        </div>
      </div>`;

    function planCard(p) {
      if (p.plan === 'free') {
        return `<div class="m-card" style="margin-bottom:12px;background:linear-gradient(150deg,rgba(255,212,121,.13),var(--m-surface));border-color:rgba(255,212,121,.35)">

          </div></div>`;
      }
      const exp = p.plan_expires_at ? new Date(p.plan_expires_at).toLocaleDateString('ar') : '';
      return `<div class="m-card" style="margin-bottom:12px;background:linear-gradient(150deg,rgba(61,220,132,.14),var(--m-surface));border-color:rgba(61,220,132,.4)">
        <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;gap:10px">
          <div><b style="font-size:13.5px">${planName(p.plan)} مفعّلة 🎉</b>
            <p style="color:var(--m-muted);font-size:11.5px;margin:4px 0 0">${exp ? 'تنتهي في ' + exp : 'عضو مميز'}</p></div>
          <span style="font-size:26px">${p.plan === 'legendary' ? '👑' : '💎'}</span>
        </div></div>`;
    }
  })();
</script>

import Base from '~/layouts/Base.astro';

<script>
  import { supabase, supabaseReady } from '~/lib/supabase.js';
  import { esc, getSession, toast, contentCardHTML, rankClass, planName } from '~/lib/mobile.js';

  const app = document.querySelector('#itemApp');
  const slug = new URLSearchParams(location.search).get('slug');

  (async () => {
    if (!supabaseReady) { app.innerHTML = '<div class="m-empty"><b>خطأ في الإعداد</b></div>'; return; }
    if (!slug) { app.innerHTML = '<div class="m-empty"><b>لا يوجد محتوى</b></div>'; return; }
    const { data: item } = await supabase.from('content_items').select('*,profiles:created_by(username,role,plan)').eq('slug', slug).eq('is_published', true).maybeSingle();
    if (!item) { app.innerHTML = '<div class="m-empty"><span class="m-empty-ico">🧩</span><b>غير موجود</b><p>ربما حُذف المحتوى أو لم يُنشر بعد</p></div>'; return; }

    supabase.rpc('record_content_view', { item_id: item.id }).catch(() => {});
    const session = await getSession();
    let isFav = false;
    if (session) {
      const { data: fav } = await supabase.from('content_favorites').select('content_id').eq('user_id', session.user.id).eq('content_id', item.id).maybeSingle();
      isFav = !!fav;
    }

    const author = item.profiles;
    const similar = await supabase.from('content_items').select('*').eq('content_type', item.content_type).eq('is_published', true).neq('id', item.id).order('download_count', { ascending: false }).limit(6);
    const rel = item.plan === 'legendary' ? '👑 Legendary' : item.plan === 'epic' ? '💎 Epic' : '🍃 Free';
    const relC = item.plan === 'legendary' ? 'legendary' : item.plan === 'epic' ? 'epic' : 'free';

    app.innerHTML = `
      <div class="m-anim">
        <div style="position:relative;height:195px;border-radius:20px;overflow:hidden;border:1px solid var(--m-border)">
          <img src="${esc(item.cover_url || '/imgs/project-logo.png')}" style="width:100%;height:100%;object-fit:cover" alt=""/>
          <div style="position:absolute;inset:0;background:linear-gradient(180deg,rgba(11,15,20,.05) 40%,rgba(11,15,20,.92))"></div>
          <div style="position:absolute;top:10px;right:10px;left:10px;display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center">
            <button class="m-chat-back" onclick="history.back()">→</button>
            <span class="m-rank ${relC}" style="background:rgba(10,14,19,.8)">${rel}</span>
          </div>
          <div style="position:absolute;bottom:10px;right:14px;left:14px">
            <div style="font-size:10px;color:var(--m-green);letter-spacing:2px">${item.content_type === 'world' ? '🌍 WORLD' : '🧩 ADDON'}</div>
            <h1 style="margin:4px 0 0;font-size:20px;font-family:var(--m-font);text-shadow:2px 2px 0 rgba(0,0,0,.7)">${esc(item.title)}</h1>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div style="display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(3,1fr);gap:8px;margin:12px 0">
          <div class="m-stat"><b>⭐ ${Number(item.rating_average || 0).toFixed(1)}</b><small>${item.rating_count || 0} تقييم</small></div>
          <div class="m-stat"><b>◉ ${item.view_count || 0}</b><small>مشاهدة</small></div>
          <div class="m-stat"><b>↓ ${item.download_count || 0}</b><small>تحميل</small></div>
        </div>

        ${author ? `<a class="m-list-item" href="/m/user?id=${author.id}">
          <span class="li-icon">👤</span>
          <span class="li-body"><span class="li-title">بواسطة ${esc(author.username)}</span>
          <span class="li-sub">${author.role === 'creator' ? 'Creator ✦' : author.role === 'admin' ? 'TheKing ✦' : planName(author.plan)}</span></span>
          <span class="li-end">عرض ←</span>
        </a>` : ''}

        <div class="m-card" style="margin:12px 0">
          <b style="font-size:13px">الوصف</b>
          <p style="color:var(--m-muted);font-size:13px;line-height:1.8;margin:8px 0 0;white-space:pre-wrap">${esc(item.description || 'لا يوجد وصف.')}</p>
        </div>

        <div style="display:flex;gap:9px;margin:4px 0 14px">
          <button class="m-btn m-btn-green" style="flex:2" id="btnDownload">📥 تحميل</button>
          <button class="m-btn ${isFav ? 'm-btn-gold' : 'm-btn-ghost'}" style="flex:1" id="btnFav">${isFav ? '★ محفوظ' : '☆ حفظ'}</button>
        </div>

        <div class="m-card-h"><h2>قد يعجبك أيضًا</h2></div>
        <div class="m-hscroll" id="mSimilar">${(similar.data || []).map((it) => contentCardHTML(it, true)).join('') || '<div class="m-empty" style="padding:14px"><p>لا يوجد محتوى مشابه</p></div>'}</div>
      </div>`;

    document.querySelector('#btnFav').onclick = async () => {
      if (!session) { toast('سجل الدخول أولًا 🔐'); setTimeout(() => location.href = '/m/login', 900); return; }
      if (isFav) {
        await supabase.from('content_favorites').delete().eq('user_id', session.user.id).eq('content_id', item.id);
        isFav = false;
      } else {
        await supabase.from('content_favorites').insert({ user_id: session.user.id, content_id: item.id });
        isFav = true;
      }
      const b = document.querySelector('#btnFav');
      b.textContent = isFav ? '★ محفوظ' : '☆ حفظ';
      b.className = 'm-btn ' + (isFav ? 'm-btn-gold' : 'm-btn-ghost');
      toast(isFav ? 'أُضيف إلى المفضلة ⭐' : 'أُزيل من المفضلة');
    };

    document.querySelector('#btnDownload').onclick = async () => {
      if (!item.file_path) { toast('ملف التحميل غير مرفوع بعد'); return; }
      if (!session) { toast('سجل الدخول أولًا 🔐'); setTimeout(() => location.href = '/m/login', 900); return; }
      const { error } = await supabase.rpc('record_content_download', { item_id: item.id });
      if (error) { toast(error.message.includes('Login') ? 'سجل الدخول أولًا 🔐' : 'تعذر التحميل'); return; }
      const { data: signed, error: signError } = await supabase.storage.from('content-files').createSignedUrl(item.file_path, 60);
      if (signError) { toast('لا تملك صلاحية تحميل هذا المحتوى'); return; }
      toast('جارٍ التحميل... 📥');
      location.href = signed.signedUrl;
    };
  })();
</script>

import Base from '~/layouts/Base.astro';
import AppShell from '~/components/mobile/AppShell.astro';

<Base noNav noFooter>
  <AppShell>
    <div id="addonsApp">
      <div class="m-loader"></div>
    </div>
  </AppShell>
</Base>

<script>
  import { supabase, supabaseReady } from '~/lib/supabase.js';
  import { esc, contentCardHTML } from '~/lib/mobile.js';

  const app = document.querySelector('#addonsApp');
  const params = new URLSearchParams(location.search);
  const initialType = params.get('type') || 'addon';
  const initialQ = params.get('q') || '';

  let type = initialType === 'world' ? 'world' : 'addon';
  let sort = 'newest';
  let query = initialQ;
  let items = [];

  const SORTS = [
    ['newest', 'الأحدث'], ['downloads', 'الأكثر تحميلًا'], ['views', 'الأكثر مشاهدة'], ['rating', 'الأعلى تقييمًا']
  ];

  async function load() {
    const field = sort === 'downloads' ? 'download_count' : sort === 'views' ? 'view_count' : sort === 'rating' ? 'rating_average' : 'created_at';
    let req = supabase.from('content_items').select('*').eq('content_type', type).eq('is_published', true).order(field, { ascending: false }).limit(60);
    if (query.trim()) req = req.ilike('title', `%${query.trim()}%`);
    const { data } = await req;
    items = data || [];
    render();
  }

  function render() {
    const grid = document.querySelector('#mGrid');
    grid.innerHTML = items.length
      ? items.map(contentCardHTML).join('')
      : '<div class="m-empty" style="grid-column:1/-1"><span class="m-empty-ico">🧩</span><b>لا توجد نتائج</b><p>جرّب كلمة بحث مختلفة أو تصفح الأقسام</p></div>';
  }

  (async () => {
    if (!supabaseReady) { app.innerHTML = '<div class="m-empty"><b>لم يتم إعداد Supabase</b></div>'; return; }
    app.innerHTML = `
      <div class="m-tabs">
        <button class="m-tab ${type === 'addon' ? 'active' : ''}" data-type="addon">🧩 إضافات</button>
        <button class="m-tab ${type === 'world' ? 'active' : ''}" data-type="world">🌍 عوالم</button>
      </div>
      <div class="m-search" style="margin-bottom:10px">
        <span class="m-search-ico">🔍</span>
        <input id="mSearch" placeholder="ابحث..." value="${esc(initialQ)}"/>
      </div>
      <div class="m-chips" id="mChips">
        ${SORTS.map(([id, label]) => `<button class="m-chip ${sort === id ? 'active' : ''}" data-sort="${id}">${label}</button>`).join('')}
      </div>
      <div class="m-grid" id="mGrid"></div>`;

    app.querySelectorAll('.m-tab').forEach((b) => b.onclick = () => {
      type = b.dataset.type;
      document.querySelectorAll('.m-tab').forEach((x) => x.classList.toggle('active', x === b));
      load();
    });
    app.querySelectorAll('[data-sort]').forEach((b) => b.onclick = () => {
      sort = b.dataset.sort;
      document.querySelectorAll('[data-sort]').forEach((x) => x.classList.toggle('active', x === b));
      load();
    });
    const search = document.querySelector('#mSearch');
    let timer;
    search.addEventListener('input', () => {
      clearTimeout(timer);
      timer = setTimeout(() => { query = search.value; load(); }, 400);
    });

    await load();
  })();
</script>

import Base from '~/layouts/Base.astro';

<Base noNav noFooter>
  <AppShell>
    <div id="profApp">
      <div class="m-loader"></div>

<script>
  import { supabase, supabaseReady } from '~/lib/supabase.js';
  import { esc, getSession, rankLabel, rankClass, contentCardHTML, planName, toast } from '~/lib/mobile.js';

  const app = document.querySelector('#profApp');
  const TAB = new URLSearchParams(location.search).get('tab') || 'about';

  (async () => {
    if (!supabaseReady) return;
    const session = await getSession();
    if (!session) { location.href = '/m/login'; return; }
    const { data: p } = await supabase.from('profiles').select('*').eq('id', session.user.id).maybeSingle();
    if (!p) { location.href = '/m/login'; return; }

    const [favs, mine, downloads] = await Promise.all([
      supabase.from('content_favorites').select('content_items(*)').eq('user_id', session.user.id),
      supabase.from('content_items').select('*').eq('created_by', session.user.id).eq('is_published', true).order('created_at', { ascending: false }).limit(30),
      supabase.from('content_downloads').select('content_items(*)').order('created_at', { ascending: false }).limit(30)
    ]);
    const favItems = (favs.data || []).map((f) => f.content_items).filter(Boolean);
    const myItems = mine.data || [];
    const dlItems = (downloads.data || []).map((d) => d.content_items).filter(Boolean);

    const joined = new Date(p.created_at).toLocaleDateString('ar');
    const isAdmin = p.role === 'admin' || p.role === 'moderator';

    app.innerHTML = `
      <div class="m-anim">
        <div class="m-cover"><img src="${esc(p.banner_url || '/imgs/page-backgrounds/worlds.webp')}" alt=""/></div>
        <div class="m-prof-head">
          <div style="position:relative;width:fit-content">
            <img class="m-prof-ava" src="${esc(p.avatar_url || '/imgs/profiles/onefree.png')}" alt=""/>
            <span class="m-prof-status ${p.show_online_status !== false ? 'on' : 'off'}"></span>
          </div>
          <h1 class="m-prof-name">${esc(p.username)} <span class="m-rank ${rankClass(p)}">${rankLabel(p)}</span></h1>
          <p class="m-prof-handle">@${esc(p.username.toLowerCase())} · عضو منذ ${joined}</p>
          <div class="m-prof-actions">
            <a class="m-btn m-btn-green" href="/m/settings">✏️ تعديل الملف</a>
            <a class="m-btn m-btn-gold" href="/m/profile?tab=plan">${p.plan === 'free' ? '👑 ترقية' : planName(p.plan)}</a>
            ${isAdmin ? `<a class="m-btn m-btn-blue" href="/admin">⚙️ لوحة</a>` : ''}
          </div>
        </div>

        <div class="m-prof-stats">
          <div class="m-stat"><b>${p.user_level || 1}</b><small>المستوى</small></div>
          <div class="m-stat"><b>${myItems.length}</b><small>محتواي</small></div>
          <div class="m-stat"><b>${favItems.length}</b><small>المفضلة</small></div>
          <div class="m-stat"><b>${dlItems.length}</b><small>تحميلاتي</small></div>
        </div>

        <div class="m-tabs">
          <button class="m-tab ${TAB === 'about' ? 'active' : ''}" data-tab="about">نبذة</button>
          <button class="m-tab ${TAB === 'content' ? 'active' : ''}" data-tab="content">محتواي</button>
          <button class="m-tab ${TAB === 'favs' ? 'active' : ''}" data-tab="favs">مفضلتي</button>
          <button class="m-tab ${TAB === 'plan' ? 'active' : ''}" data-tab="plan">خطتي</button>
        </div>
        <div id="tabBody"></div>
      </div>`;

    const body = document.querySelector('#tabBody');
    function renderTab(tab) {
      document.querySelectorAll('.m-tab').forEach((b) => b.classList.toggle('active', b.dataset.tab === tab));
      if (tab === 'about') {
        body.innerHTML = `
          <div class="m-card" style="margin-bottom:10px">
            <b style="font-size:13px">📝 النبذة</b>
            <p style="color:var(--m-muted);font-size:13px;line-height:1.8;margin:8px 0 0">${esc(p.bio || 'لا توجد نبذة بعد.')}</p>
          </div>
          <div class="m-card" style="margin-bottom:10px">
            <b style="font-size:13px">⚡ معلومات الحساب</b>
            <div style="margin-top:10px;display:grid;gap:9px;font-size:12.5px;color:var(--m-muted)">
              <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between"><span>الخطة</span><span class="m-rank ${rankClass(p)}">${rankLabel(p)}</span></div>
              <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between"><span>حالة الظهور</span><span>${p.show_online_status !== false ? '🟢 متصل' : '⚪ مخفي'}</span></div>
              <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between"><span>الأداة بجانب الاسم</span><span>${esc(p.equipped_item_id === 'none' || !p.equipped_item_id ? 'بدون' : p.equipped_item_id)}</span></div>
              <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between"><span>البريد</span><span dir="ltr" style="max-width:55%;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis">${esc(session.user.email || '')}</span></div>
            </div>
          </div>
          <a class="m-btn m-btn-ghost m-btn-block" href="/m/settings" style="margin-top:4px">الإعدادات الكاملة ⚙️</a>
          <button class="m-btn m-btn-danger m-btn-block" id="btnLogout" style="margin-top:8px">تسجيل الخروج</button>`;
        document.querySelector('#btnLogout').onclick = async () => {
          await supabase.auth.signOut();
          location.href = '/m/login';
        };
      } else if (tab === 'content') {
        body.innerHTML = myItems.length
          ? `<div class="m-grid">${myItems.map(contentCardHTML).join('')}</div>
             <a class="m-btn m-btn-ghost m-btn-block" href="/my-addons" style="margin-top:10px">إدارة المحتوى على الكمبيوتر</a>`
          : `<div class="m-empty"><span class="m-empty-ico">📦</span><b>لا يوجد محتوى بعد</b><p>أنشئ إضافاتك وعوالمك من لوحة الإدارة</p></div>`;
      } else if (tab === 'favs') {
        body.innerHTML = favItems.length
          ? `<div class="m-grid">${favItems.map(contentCardHTML).join('')}</div>`
          : `<div class="m-empty"><span class="m-empty-ico">⭐</span><b>لا توجد مفضلة بعد</b><p>اضغط ☆ على أي محتوى لحفظه هنا</p></div>`;
      } else {
        body.innerHTML = `
          <div class="m-card" style="margin-bottom:10px;${p.plan !== 'free' ? 'border-color:rgba(61,220,132,.45)' : 'border-color:rgba(255,212,121,.35)'}">
            <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center">
              <div><b style="font-size:15px">${p.plan === 'free' ? 'الخطة المجانية 🍃' : planName(p.plan) + ' مفعّلة 🎉'}</b>
              <p style="color:var(--m-muted);font-size:12px;margin:5px 0 0">${p.plan_expires_at ? 'تنتهي في ' + new Date(p.plan_expires_at).toLocaleDateString('ar') : p.plan === 'free' ? 'أكثر من 300 أفاتار وأداة بجانب الاسم' : 'مزايا كاملة حتى الآن'}</p></div>
              <span style="font-size:32px">${p.plan === 'legendary' ? '👑' : p.plan === 'epic' ? '💎' : '🍃'}</span>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div style="display:grid;gap:9px;margin-bottom:10px">
            ${[['🍃 Free', 'الأساسيات المجانية', 'مجاني', p.plan === 'free' ? 'الحالية' : 'تغيير'], ['💎 Epic', 'أفاتارات مميزة + كل الأساسيات · 25 د.ل / 30 يوم', 'ترقية', p.plan === 'epic' ? 'الحالية' : 'اشترِ'], ['👑 Legendary', 'كل شيء + أداة ذهبية بجانب اسمك · 50 د.ل / 30 يوم', 'ترقية', p.plan === 'legendary' ? 'الحالية' : 'اشترِ']].map(([t, d, act, state]) => `
              <div class="m-card" style="padding:13px;${p.plan === t.split(' ')[0].replace('🍃', 'free').replace('💎', 'epic').replace('👑', 'legendary') ? 'border-color:var(--m-green)' : ''}">
                <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;gap:8px">
                  <div><b style="font-size:13px">${t}</b><p style="color:var(--m-muted);font-size:11px;margin:3px 0 0">${d}</p></div>
                  <span class="m-rank ${t.includes('💎') ? 'epic' : t.includes('👑') ? 'legendary' : 'free'}" style="font-size:11px">${state}</span>
                </div>
              </div>`).join('')}
          </div>
          <a class="m-btn m-btn-gold m-btn-block" href="/checkout">شراء اشتراك 💳</a>
          <p style="color:var(--m-dim);font-size:11px;text-align:center;margin-top:8px">الدفع: OnePay / LPay / Edfali / MobiCash / تحويل بنكي</p>`;
      }
    }
    document.querySelectorAll('.m-tab').forEach((b) => b.onclick = () => renderTab(b.dataset.tab));
    renderTab(TAB);
  })();
</script>

---
import Base from '~/layouts/Base.astro';
import AppShell from '~/components/mobile/AppShell.astro';
---
<Base noNav noFooter>
  <AppShell>
    <div id="notifApp">
      <div class="m-loader"></div>
    </div>
  </AppShell>
</Base>

<script>
  import { supabase, supabaseReady } from '~/lib/supabase.js';
  import { esc, getSession, fmtTime, toast } from '~/lib/mobile.js';

  const app = document.querySelector('#notifApp');

  async function load() {
    const { data: list } = await supabase.from('chat_notifications')
      .select('*')
      .eq('recipient_id', (await getSession()).user.id)
      .order('created_at', { ascending: false }).limit(50);

    const actorIds = [...new Set((list || []).map((n) => n.actor_id).filter(Boolean))];
    const msgIds = [...new Set((list || []).map((n) => n.message_id).filter(Boolean))];
    let actors = {}, msgInfo = {};
    if (actorIds.length) { const { data: ps } = await supabase.from('profiles').select('id,username,avatar_url').in('id', actorIds); (ps || []).forEach((p) => actors[p.id] = p); }
    if (msgIds.length) { const { data: ms } = await supabase.from('chat_messages').select('id,channel_id').in('id', msgIds); (ms || []).forEach((m) => msgInfo[m.id] = m); }

    const container = document.querySelector('#notifList');
    container.innerHTML = (list && list.length) ? list.map((n) => {
      const actor = actors[n.actor_id];
      let icon = '🔔', text = '';
      if (n.notification_type === 'friend_request') { icon = '🤝'; text = 'أرسل لك طلب صداقة'; }
      else if (n.notification_type === 'mention') { icon = '💬'; text = 'ذكرك في رسالة'; }
      else if (n.notification_type === 'reply') { icon = '↩️'; text = 'رد على رسالتك'; }
      else if (n.notification_type === 'thread') { icon = '🧵'; text = 'شارك في سلسلتك'; }
      const ch = msgInfo[n.message_id]?.channel_id;
      return `<div class="m-list-item" data-nid="${n.id}" ${ch ? `data-open="${ch}" data-msg="${n.message_id}"` : ''}>
        ${actor ? `<img src="${esc(actor.avatar_url || '/imgs/profiles/onefree.png')}" style="width:42px;height:42px;border-radius:50%;object-fit:cover;flex:0 0 auto"/>` : `<span class="li-icon">${icon}</span>`}
        <span class="li-body"><span class="li-title"><b style="color:var(--m-text)">${esc(actor?.username || 'مستخدم')}</b> ${text}</span>
        <span class="li-sub">${fmtTime(n.created_at)}${n.is_read ? '' : ' · <span style="color:var(--m-green)">جديد</span>'}</span></span>
        ${n.notification_type === 'friend_request' && n.actor_id ? `<span class="li-end" style="display:flex;gap:6px">
          <button class="m-btn m-btn-green" style="min-height:32px;padding:5px 12px;font-size:11px" data-accept="${n.actor_id}">قبول</button>
        </span>` : ch ? '<span class="li-end">فتح ←</span>' : ''}
      </div>`;
    }).join('') : '<div class="m-empty"><span class="m-empty-ico">🔕</span><b>لا توجد تنبيهات</b><p>عندما يذكرك أحد أو يرسل لك طلب صداقة، سيظهر هنا</p></div>';

    container.querySelectorAll('[data-accept]').forEach((b) => b.onclick = async (e) => {
      e.stopPropagation();
      await supabase.from('user_friends').update({ status: 'accepted' }).eq('requester_id', b.dataset.accept).eq('receiver_id', (await getSession()).user.id);
      toast('تمت إضافة الصديق 🎉');
      b.textContent = '✓ تم';
      b.disabled = true;
    });
    container.querySelectorAll('[data-open]').forEach((el) => el.onclick = () => {
      location.href = `/m/community?channel=${el.dataset.open}`;
    });
  }

  (async () => {
    if (!supabaseReady) return;
    const session = await getSession();
    if (!session) { location.href = '/m/login'; return; }
    app.innerHTML = `
      <div class="m-card-h" style="margin-top:2px"><h2>🔔 التنبيهات</h2></div>
      <div id="notifList"></div>`;
    await load();
    supabase.from('chat_notifications').update({ is_read: true }).eq('recipient_id', session.user.id).eq('is_read', false).then(() => {});
  })();
</script>

import Base from '~/layouts/Base.astro';

      <div class="m-loader"></div>

<script>
  import { supabase, supabaseReady } from '~/lib/supabase.js';
  import { chatItems } from '~/data/chat-items.js';
  import { esc, getSession, toast } from '~/lib/mobile.js';

  const app = document.querySelector('#setApp');

  (async () => {
    if (!supabaseReady) return;
    const session = await getSession();
    if (!session) { location.href = '/m/login'; return; }
    const { data: p } = await supabase.from('profiles').select('*').eq('id', session.user.id).maybeSingle();
    if (!p) { location.href = '/m/login'; return; }

    const { data: assets } = await supabase.from('site_assets').select('*').eq('enabled', true);
    const avatars = [{ id: 'default', url: '/imgs/profiles/onefree.png', required_plan: 'free' }, ...(assets || []).filter((a) => a.asset_type === 'avatar').map((a) => ({ id: a.id, url: a.file_url, required_plan: a.required_plan }))];
    const items = chatItems.filter((i) => i.enabled);

    app.innerHTML = `
      <div class="m-card-h" style="margin-top:2px"><h2>⚙️ الإعدادات</h2></div>

      <div class="m-card" style="margin-bottom:10px">
        <b style="font-size:13px">الملف الشخصي</b>
        <label style="display:block;margin-top:12px;font-size:12px;color:var(--m-muted)">اسم المستخدم</label>
        <input class="m-input" id="fUsername" value="${esc(p.username)}" style="margin-top:6px"/>
        <label style="display:block;margin-top:12px;font-size:12px;color:var(--m-muted)">النبذة</label>
        <textarea class="m-input" id="fBio" rows="3" style="margin-top:6px;resize:vertical;min-height:80px">${esc(p.bio || '')}</textarea>
        <button class="m-btn m-btn-green m-btn-block" id="btnSave" style="margin-top:12px">حفظ التعديلات</button>
      </div>

      <div class="m-card" style="margin-bottom:10px">
        <b style="font-size:13px">الأفاتار ${p.plan === 'free' ? '· 🔒 بعضها للـ Epic+' : ''}</b>
        <div style="display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(4,1fr);gap:8px;margin-top:12px">
          ${avatars.map((a) => {
            const locked = a.required_plan !== 'free' && p.plan === 'free';
            return `<button class="m-ava-opt ${a.url === p.avatar_url ? 'sel' : ''} ${locked ? 'locked' : ''}" data-ava="${esc(a.url)}" ${locked ? 'disabled' : ''}>
              <img src="${esc(a.url)}" alt=""/><i>${locked ? '🔒' : '✓'}</i></button>`;
          }).join('')}
        </div>
      </div>

      <div class="m-card" style="margin-bottom:10px">
        <b style="font-size:13px">الأداة بجانب الاسم في الشات</b>
        <div style="display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(5,1fr);gap:8px;margin-top:12px">
          ${items.map((it) => {
            const locked = it.requiredPlan !== 'free' && p.plan === 'free' && it.requiredPlan !== 'admin';
            const adminOnly = it.requiredPlan === 'admin' && p.role !== 'admin';
            const dis = locked || adminOnly;
            const sel = p.equipped_item_id === it.id;
            return `<button class="m-item-opt ${sel ? 'sel' : ''} ${dis ? 'locked' : ''}" data-item="${it.id}" ${dis ? 'disabled' : ''} title="${esc(it.nameAr)}">
              ${it.image ? `<img src="${it.image}" alt=""/>` : '<span style="font-size:20px">🚫</span>'}<i>${dis ? '🔒' : '✓'}</i></button>`;
          }).join('')}
        </div>
      </div>

      <div class="m-card" style="margin-bottom:10px">
        <b style="font-size:13px">الخصوصية</b>
        <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;margin-top:10px">
          <span style="font-size:12.5px;color:var(--m-muted)">إظهار حالة "متصل الآن" ${p.plan === 'free' ? '<span class="m-rank epic" style="margin-right:4px">Epic+</span>' : ''}</span>
          <button id="btnStatus" class="m-chip ${p.show_online_status !== false ? 'active' : ''}" style="min-width:70px">${p.show_online_status !== false ? '🟢 ظاهر' : '⚪ مخفي'}</button>
        </div>
      </div>

      <button class="m-btn m-btn-danger m-btn-block" id="btnLogout" style="margin-top:6px">تسجيل الخروج</button>
      <p style="color:var(--m-dim);font-size:11px;text-align:center;margin-top:10px">AddonStation Mobile v1.2 · ${esc(session.user.email || '')}</p>`;

    app.querySelectorAll('.m-ava-opt').forEach((b) => b.onclick = async () => {
      const { error } = await supabase.from('profiles').update({ avatar_url: b.dataset.ava }).eq('id', session.user.id);
      if (error) { toast(error.message); return; }
      app.querySelectorAll('.m-ava-opt').forEach((x) => x.classList.toggle('sel', x === b));
      toast('تم تغيير الأفاتار 🎨');
    });
    app.querySelectorAll('.m-item-opt').forEach((b) => b.onclick = async () => {
      const { error } = await supabase.from('profiles').update({ equipped_item_id: b.dataset.item }).eq('id', session.user.id);
      if (error) { toast(error.message); return; }
      app.querySelectorAll('.m-item-opt').forEach((x) => x.classList.toggle('sel', x === b));
      toast('تم تحديث الأداة ✨');
    });
    document.querySelector('#btnSave').onclick = async () => {
      const username = document.querySelector('#fUsername').value.trim();
      const bio = document.querySelector('#fBio').value.trim();
      if (username.length < 3) { toast('اسم المستخدم 3 أحرف على الأقل'); return; }
      const { error } = await supabase.from('profiles').update({ username, bio }).eq('id', session.user.id);
      if (error) { toast(error.message); return; }
      toast('تم حفظ التعديلات ✅');
    };
    document.querySelector('#btnStatus').onclick = async () => {
      const next = p.show_online_status === false;
      const { error } = await supabase.from('profiles').update({ show_online_status: next }).eq('id', session.user.id);
      if (error) { toast(error.message); return; }
      p.show_online_status = next;
      const b = document.querySelector('#btnStatus');
      b.textContent = next ? '🟢 ظاهر' : '⚪ مخفي';
      b.classList.toggle('active', next);
      toast(next ? 'أنت الآن ظاهر في قائمة المتصلين' : 'أصبحت مخفيًا الآن');
    };
    document.querySelector('#btnLogout').onclick = async () => {
      await supabase.auth.signOut();
      location.href = '/m/login';
    };
  })();
</script>

  .m-ava-opt img,.m-item-opt img{width:100%;height:100%;object-fit:cover}

import Base from '~/layouts/Base.astro';

<script>
  import { supabase, supabaseReady } from '~/lib/supabase.js';
  import { esc, getSession, rankLabel, rankClass, contentCardHTML, toast } from '~/lib/mobile.js';

  const app = document.querySelector('#userApp');
  const id = new URLSearchParams(location.search).get('id');

  (async () => {
    if (!supabaseReady) return;
    const session = await getSession();
    if (!session) { location.href = '/m/login'; return; }
    if (!id) { app.innerHTML = '<div class="m-empty"><b>مستخدم غير محدد</b></div>'; return; }

    const { data: p } = await supabase.from('profiles').select('*').eq('id', id).maybeSingle();
    if (!p) { app.innerHTML = '<div class="m-empty"><span class="m-empty-ico">👤</span><b>غير موجود</b></div>'; return; }

    const [myItems, isFriend] = await Promise.all([
      supabase.from('content_items').select('*').eq('created_by', id).eq('is_published', true).order('created_at', { ascending: false }).limit(24),
      supabase.from('user_friends').select('status').eq('requester_id', session.user.id).eq('receiver_id', id).maybeSingle()
    ]);

    const isMe = session.user.id === id;
    const joined = new Date(p.created_at).toLocaleDateString('ar');

    app.innerHTML = `
      <div class="m-anim">
        <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;margin-bottom:10px">
          <button class="m-chat-back" onclick="history.back()">→</button>
          <b style="font-family:var(--m-font);font-size:13px">الملف الشخصي</b>
        </div>
        <div class="m-cover"><img src="${esc(p.banner_url || '/imgs/page-backgrounds/worlds.webp')}" alt=""/></div>
        <div class="m-prof-head">
          <div style="position:relative;width:fit-content">
            <img class="m-prof-ava" src="${esc(p.avatar_url || '/imgs/profiles/onefree.png')}" alt=""/>
            <span class="m-prof-status ${p.show_online_status !== false ? 'on' : 'off'}"></span>
          </div>
          <h1 class="m-prof-name">${esc(p.username)} <span class="m-rank ${rankClass(p)}">${rankLabel(p)}</span></h1>
          <p class="m-prof-handle">@${esc(p.username.toLowerCase())} · عضو منذ ${joined}</p>
          <div class="m-prof-actions">
            ${!isMe ? `<button class="m-btn ${isFriend?.data?.status === 'accepted' ? 'm-btn-ghost' : 'm-btn-blue'}" id="btnFriend">${isFriend?.data?.status === 'accepted' ? '✓ صديق' : isFriend?.data?.status === 'pending' ? '⏳ بانتظار الرد' : '🤝 إضافة صديق'}</button>
            <button class="m-btn m-btn-green" id="btnMention">💬 ذكر</button>` : '<a class="m-btn m-btn-green" style="flex:1" href="/m/profile">هذا أنت ✨</a>'}
          </div>
        </div>

        <div class="m-card" style="margin:12px 0 14px">
          <b style="font-size:13px">📝 النبذة</b>
          <p style="color:var(--m-muted);font-size:13px;line-height:1.8;margin:8px 0 0">${esc(p.bio || 'لا توجد نبذة.')}</p>
          <div style="margin-top:10px;padding-top:10px;border-top:1px solid var(--m-border);display:flex;justify-content:space-around;text-align:center">
            <div><b style="display:block;font-family:var(--m-font)">${p.user_level || 1}</b><small style="color:var(--m-muted);font-size:10px">المستوى</small></div>
            <div><b style="display:block;font-family:var(--m-font)">${(myItems.data || []).length}</b><small style="color:var(--m-muted);font-size:10px">محتوى</small></div>
            <div><b style="display:block;font-family:var(--m-font)">${p.plan === 'free' ? '🍃' : p.plan === 'epic' ? '💎' : '👑'}</b><small style="color:var(--m-muted);font-size:10px">الخطة</small></div>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div class="m-card-h"><h2>${(myItems.data || []).length ? '📦 محتواه' : '📦 لا يوجد محتوى بعد'}</h2></div>
        <div class="m-grid">${(myItems.data || []).map(contentCardHTML).join('')}</div>
      </div>`;

    const friendBtn = document.querySelector('#btnFriend');
    if (friendBtn && !isMe) friendBtn.onclick = async () => {
      if (isFriend?.data) {
        if (isFriend.data.status === 'pending') { toast('الطلب قيد الانتظار'); return; }
        if (isFriend.data.status === 'accepted') { toast('أنتما صديقان بالفعل 🎉'); return; }
      }
      await supabase.from('user_friends').upsert({ requester_id: session.user.id, receiver_id: id, status: 'pending' });
      await supabase.from('chat_notifications').insert({ recipient_id: id, actor_id: session.user.id, notification_type: 'friend_request' });
      friendBtn.textContent = '⏳ بانتظار الرد';
      friendBtn.className = 'm-btn m-btn-ghost';
      toast('أُرسل طلب الصداقة 🤝');
    };
    const mentionBtn = document.querySelector('#btnMention');
    if (mentionBtn) mentionBtn.onclick = () => { sessionStorage.setItem('m_mention', p.username); location.href = '/m/community'; };
  })();
</script>
